#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json,sys,os
P=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__),"Crows-432.json")
def main(a):
 d=json.load(open(P,encoding="utf-8"))
 if not a or a[0]=="path": print(P); return 0
 if a[0]=="rows": print(json.dumps(d["rows"],indent=1,ensure_ascii=False)); return 0
 if a[0]=="query" and len(a)>1:
  q=a[1]; m=[r for r in d["rows"] if q in r["skill"] or q in r["apply"] or q in r["result"]]
  print(json.dumps(m,indent=1,ensure_ascii=False)); return 0 if m else 1
 print("usage: preview-432-r2.py [path|rows|query <text>]"); return 2
if __name__=="__main__": sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

The fenced python block below is ONE contiguous, complete block; it contains no headings, prose or signature lines. **Extraction/execution:** read the lines strictly between the opening and closing ~~~ fences of this section into a temp verifier-432.py, then run `python verifier-432.py <this-record.md>`. ~~~python
import sys,re,json,hashlib
BT=chr(96)
EXPECTED='VERIFIER OK rows=2667 unique=2667 malformed=0 classes i180/ii41/iii64/iv529/v1853 disps11/157/2499 exclusions=2499 per-page=8 Crows.json=full-totals+rows-agree raw-angle=533 inline-artifacts=6/6 stale=0 span=ok duplicate-token=ok support-cli=ok stage-a=ok row-source=ok crows-full=ok disk-support=ok stale-marker=ok'
errs=[]
def err(m): errs.append(m)
if len(sys.argv)!=2:
 print('usage: python verifier-432.py <record.md>'); sys.exit(2)
try:
 text=open(sys.argv[1],encoding='utf-8').read(); lines=text.split(chr(10))
except Exception as e:
 print('VERIFIER FAIL: cannot read record:',e); sys.exit(2)
rows=[]; malformed=0; intable=False
for l in lines:
 if l.startswith('<!-- candidate-inventory:start -->'): intable=True; continue
 if l.startswith('<!-- candidate-inventory:end -->'): intable=False; continue
 if not intable or not l.startswith('| P4'): continue
 m=re.match(r'^\| (P\d{3}) \| (\d+)@(\d+) \| ([iv]+) \| (.+?) \| (.+?) \|$',l)
 if not m: malformed+=1; continue
 pg,ln,off,cls,tok,disp=m.groups(); rows.append((pg,int(ln),int(off),cls,tok,disp))
from collections import Counter
classes=Counter(r[3] for r in rows); disps=Counter('defined' if r[5].startswith('defined') else ('named' if r[5].startswith('named') else 'excluded') for r in rows); pp=Counter(r[0] for r in rows)
if len(rows)!=2667: err('row count %d'%len(rows))
if len({(r[0],r[1],r[2],r[4]) for r in rows})!=len(rows): err('duplicate keys')
if malformed: err('malformed rows %d'%malformed)
for k,v in {'i':180,'ii':41,'iii':64,'iv':529,'v':1853}.items():
 if classes.get(k,0)!=v: err('class %s=%d'%(k,classes.get(k,0)))
for k,v in {'defined':11,'named':157,'excluded':2499}.items():
 if disps.get(k,0)!=v: err('disposition %s=%d'%(k,disps.get(k,0)))
for p,c in [('P432',509),('P433',429),('P434',251),('P435',465),('P436',255),('P437',198),('P438',339),('P439',221)]:
 if pp.get(p,0)!=c: err('per-page %s=%d'%(p,pp.get(p,0)))
seen=set(); angle=0; codes=Counter()
for pg,ln,off,cls,tok,disp in rows:
 if tok.startswith(BT) or tok.endswith(BT):
  if not (tok.startswith(BT) and tok.endswith(BT)): err('partial backtick %r'%((pg,ln,tok),))
  if cls not in ('iv','v'): err('backticked not iv/v %r'%((pg,ln,tok),))
 if cls=='iv' and not (tok.startswith(BT) and tok.endswith(BT)): err('class iv not backticked %r'%((pg,ln,tok),))
 if '<' in tok or '>' in tok:
  angle+=1
  if not (tok.startswith(BT) and tok.endswith(BT)): err('raw angle outside code span %r'%((pg,ln,tok),))
  if not disp.startswith('documented exclusion - PLACEHOLDER'): err('placeholder disposition %r'%((pg,ln,tok),))
 d=disp.split('documented exclusion - ',1)
 if len(d)>1: codes[d[1].split(':',1)[0]]+=1
 if disp.startswith(('defined','named')):
  k=(pg,tok)
  if k in seen: err('multiple defined/named per page+token %r'%(k,))
  seen.add(k)
if angle!=533: err('raw-angle count %d'%angle)
if sum(codes.values())!=2499: err('exclusion code sum %d'%sum(codes.values()))
marker='## 11. Embedded verifier source, extraction and expected output'
if marker not in text: err('stale-scan marker missing')
scan=text.split(marker,1)[0]
scan=re.sub(r'### A-rows-ref\.txt canonical block.*?~~~.*?~~~','',scan,flags=re.S)
for bad in ['2,575','f73c629d','bd5162','6e28','3,062','e4ce1263','eb87acee','8c598973','A plan r1']:
 if bad in scan: err('stale value '+bad)
arts={}
def extract(name):
 h='### '+name+' canonical block'
 try: i=next(i for i,l in enumerate(lines) if l==h)
 except StopIteration: err('missing block '+name); return
 j=next((j for j in range(i+1,len(lines)) if lines[j].startswith('~~~')),None)
 if j is None: err('missing open fence '+name); return
 k=next((k for k in range(j+1,len(lines)) if lines[k].startswith('~~~')),None)
 if k is None: err('missing close fence '+name); return
 content=chr(10).join(lines[j+1:k])+chr(10)
 m=None
 for x in range(i+1,j):
  mm=re.search(r'\*\*Measured:\*\* (\d+) B / (\d+) LF / sha ([0-9a-f]{64})',lines[x])
  if mm: m=mm; break
 if not m: err('missing measured '+name); return
 b=content.encode(); bm=(len(b),content.count(chr(10)),hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest())
 if (int(m.group(1)),int(m.group(2)),m.group(3))!=bm: err('measured mismatch '+name)
 arts[name]={'bytes':bm[0],'lf':bm[1],'sha':bm[2],'text':content}
for a in ['A-rows-ref.txt','Crows-432.txt','Crows-432.json','README-432.md','MANIFEST-432.txt','preview-432-r2.py']: extract(a)
if len(arts)==6:
 try:
  cj=json.loads(arts['Crows-432.json']['text']); t=cj.get('totals',{})
  if len(cj.get('rows',[]))!=4: err('Crows rows')
  if t.get('rows')!=len(rows): err('Crows totals rows')
  if t.get('classes')!=dict(classes): err('Crows totals classes')
  if t.get('dispositions')!=dict(disps): err('Crows totals disps')
  if t.get('codes_sum')!=sum(codes.values()): err('Crows codes sum')
  if t.get('per_page')!=dict(pp): err('Crows per-page')
 except Exception as e: err('Crows json: '+str(e))
 try:
  if not all('apply' in r for r in cj.get('rows',[])): err('Crows rows missing apply key')
  psrc=arts['preview-432-r2.py']['text']
  if ("r['apply']" not in psrc and 'r["apply"]' not in psrc) or 'applied' in psrc: err('preview query key')
  qtext='acceptance'
  mm=[r for r in cj.get('rows',[]) if qtext in (r.get('skill','')+r.get('apply','')+r.get('result',''))]
  if not mm: err('support-cli query unmatched')
 except Exception as e: err('support-cli: '+str(e))
 read=arts['README-432.md']['text']
 for name,a in arts.items():
  if name in ('README-432.md','MANIFEST-432.txt'): continue
  mm=re.search(r'^\| '+re.escape(name)+r' \| (\d+) \| (\d+) \| ([0-9a-f]{64}) \|$',read,re.M)
  if not mm: err('README missing '+name); continue
  if (int(mm.group(1)),int(mm.group(2)),mm.group(3))!=(a['bytes'],a['lf'],a['sha']): err('README mismatch '+name)
 man=arts['MANIFEST-432.txt']['text']
 for name,a in arts.items():
  if name=='MANIFEST-432.txt': continue
  mm=re.search(r'^'+re.escape(name)+r'  sha256=([0-9a-f]{64})  bytes=(\d+)  lf=(\d+)$',man,re.M)
  if not mm: err('MANIFEST missing '+name); continue
  if (mm.group(1),int(mm.group(2)),int(mm.group(3)))!=(a['sha'],a['bytes'],a['lf']): err('MANIFEST mismatch '+name)
 try: compile(arts['preview-432-r2.py']['text'],'preview-432-r2.py','exec')
 except Exception as e: err('preview compile: '+str(e))
 try:
  if t.get('codes')!=dict(codes): err('Crows codes distribution')
  txt=arts['Crows-432.txt']['text']
  for rr in cj.get('rows',[]):
   if rr.get('skill','') not in txt: err('Crows txt/JSON skill mismatch')
   if rr.get('apply','') not in txt: err('Crows txt/JSON apply mismatch')
   if rr.get('result','') not in txt: err('Crows txt/JSON result mismatch')
  if len(cj.get('rows',[]))!=len([l for l in txt.split(chr(10)) if l.startswith('| ')]): err('Crows txt/JSON row count')
 except Exception as e: err('crows-full: '+str(e))
 sec=text[text.find('## 8. Stage A reference'):text.find('## 9. Stage C self account')]
 if 'historical r1 capture' not in sec or 'accepted plan r3' not in sec or 'sd-a-read-lc-432-r3' not in sec: err('stage-a reference')
 try:
  import subprocess, os as _os
  d=_os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(sys.argv[1])); ev=None
  for _ in range(8):
   cand=_os.path.join(d,'_A','evidence','read-lc432-c1')
   if _os.path.isdir(cand): ev=cand; break
   d=_os.path.dirname(d)
  if ev is None: err('disk evidence dir not found')
  else:
   for nm,a in arts.items():
    fp=_os.path.join(ev,nm)
    if not _os.path.isfile(fp): err('disk missing '+nm); continue
    bb=open(fp,'rb').read(); bm=(len(bb),bb.count(b'\n'),hashlib.sha256(bb).hexdigest())
    if bm!=(a['bytes'],a['lf'],a['sha']): err('disk mismatch '+nm)
   pv=_os.path.join(ev,'preview-432-r2.py')
   rr=subprocess.run([sys.executable,pv,'query','acceptance'],cwd=ev,capture_output=True,text=True)
   if rr.returncode!=0 or 'acceptance-criteria-reviewer' not in rr.stdout: err('disk support-cli query failed rc=%d'%rr.returncode)
 except Exception as e: err('disk-support: '+str(e))
 try:
  rsrcs=re.findall(r'<!-- Row source:.*?-->',text.split('## 10. Boundary')[0])
  if len(rsrcs)!=4: err('row source count %d'%len(rsrcs))
  for rs in rsrcs:
   mm=re.search(r'README-432\.md sha256 ([0-9a-f]{64})',rs)
   if not mm or mm.group(1)!=arts['README-432.md']['sha']: err('row-source README hash')
   if 'support-cli=_A/evidence/read-lc432-c1/preview-432-r2.py' not in rs: err('row-source support-cli')
 except Exception as e: err('row-source: '+str(e))

if errs:
 print('VERIFIER FAIL:')
 for e in errs: print(' -',e)
 sys.exit(1)
print(EXPECTED)

**Negative tests to reproduce:** (a) remove the code span from one angle-placeholder token -> exit1 raw-angle/placeholder; (b) change one class-iv backticked row to i -> exit1 class/backtick; (c) mutate the MANIFEST-432.txt measured sha -> exit1 measured mismatch; (d) duplicate one named token on the same page -> exit1 multiple defined/named per page+token; (e) no argument -> exit2. Each must be non-zero; never treat a pass as a merge gate.
